===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 513-541
Documentation:

Method code:
513 |     @Override
514 |     void endFile() {
515 |       super.endFile();
516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

===== M0027 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 994-1050
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
994 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
995 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
996 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
997 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
998 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
999 |     }
1000 | 
1001 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
1002 |       return false;
1003 |     }
1004 | 
1005 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
1006 | 
1007 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1008 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1009 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1010 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1011 |         return false;
1012 |       }
1013 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1014 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1015 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1016 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1017 |         return false;
1018 |       }
1019 | 
1020 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1021 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1022 |         return false;
1023 |       }
1024 | 
1025 |       // Math.floor has no side-effects.
1026 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1027 |       // I create a definitionProvider that understands namespacing.
1028 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1029 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1030 |           return false;
1031 |         }
1032 |       }
1033 | 
1034 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1035 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1036 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1037 |           return false;
1038 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1039 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1040 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1041 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1042 |           if (param != null &&
1043 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp()))
1044 |           return false;
1045 |         }
1046 |       }
1047 |     }
1048 | 
1049 |     return true;
1050 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
48 |   CodeGenerator(
49 |       CodeConsumer consumer, Charset outputCharset) {
50 |     cc = consumer;
51 |     if (outputCharset == null || outputCharset == Charsets.US_ASCII) {
52 |       // If we want our default (pretending to be UTF-8, but escaping anything
53 |       // outside of straight ASCII), then don't use the encoder, but
54 |       // just special-case the code.  This keeps the normal path through
55 |       // the code identical to how it's been for years.
56 |       this.outputCharsetEncoder = null;
57 |     } else {
58 |       this.outputCharsetEncoder = outputCharset.newEncoder();
59 |     }
60 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
710 |     @Override
711 |     Node processIfStatement(IfStatement statementNode) {
712 |       Node node = newNode(Token.IF);
713 |       node.addChildToBack(transform(statementNode.getCondition()));
714 |       node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(statementNode.getThenPart()));
715 |       if (statementNode.getElsePart() != null) {
716 |         node.addChildToBack(transformBlock(statementNode.getElsePart()));
717 |       }

===== M0059 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0063 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.addChildrenAfter(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 670-694
Documentation:
/**
   * Add all children after 'node'.
   */
Method code:
670 |   public void addChildrenAfter(Node children, Node node) {
671 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node == null || node.parent == this);
672 |     for (Node child = children; child != null; child = child.next) {
673 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(child.parent == null);
674 |       child.parent = this;
675 |     }
676 | 
677 |     Node lastSibling = children.getLastSibling();
678 |     if (node != null) {
679 |       Node oldNext = node.next;
680 |       node.next = children;
681 |       lastSibling.next = oldNext;
682 |       if (node == last) {
683 |         last = lastSibling;
684 |       }
685 |     } else {
686 |       // Append to the beginning.
687 |       if (first != null) {
688 |         lastSibling.next = first;
689 |       } else {
690 |         last = lastSibling;
691 |       }
692 |       first = children;
693 |     }
694 |   }

185 |     private void annotateDispatchers(Node n, Node parent) {
186 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
187 |       if (parent.getJSDocInfo() != null
188 |           && parent.getJSDocInfo().isJavaDispatch()) {
189 |         if (parent.isAssign()) {
190 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getLastChild() == n);
191 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_DISPATCHER, true);
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }

381 |   public Node(int nodeType, Node left, Node right) {

385 |         "first new child has existing sibling");

179 |                                           node.getClass() +

181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);

212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }

215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:

220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);

224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);

233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);

242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:

245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1051-1053
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1051 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1052 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1053 |   }

===== M0096 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 489-506
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

Method code:
221 |   private Node transformBlock(AstNode node) {
222 |     Node irNode = transform(node);
223 |     if (!irNode.isBlock()) {
224 |       if (irNode.isEmpty()) {
225 |         irNode.setType(Token.BLOCK);
226 |         irNode.setWasEmptyNode(true);
227 |       } else {
228 |         Node newBlock = newNode(Token.BLOCK, irNode);
229 |         newBlock.setLineno(irNode.getLineno());
230 |         newBlock.setCharno(irNode.getCharno());
231 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newBlock, node);
232 |         irNode = newBlock;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 |     return irNode;
236 |   }

===== M0113 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 504-524
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
504 |   @Override
505 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
506 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
507 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
508 |     if (unknown) {
509 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
510 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
511 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
512 |         unknown = false;
513 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
514 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
515 |             unknown = true;
516 |             break;
517 |           }
518 |         }
519 |       } else {
520 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |     return unknown;
524 |   }

===== M0117 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyStatement(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 799-855
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
799 |   private void addNonEmptyStatement(
800 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
801 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
802 | 
803 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && !n.isBlock()) {
804 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
805 |     }
806 | 
807 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
808 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
809 |     if (n.isBlock()) {
810 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2);
811 |       if (count == 0) {
812 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
813 |           cc.beginBlock();
814 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
815 |         } else {
816 |           cc.endStatement(true);
817 |         }
818 |         return;
819 |       }
820 | 
821 |       if (count == 1) {
822 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
823 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
824 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
825 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
826 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
827 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock || isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(firstAndOnlyChild)) {
828 |           cc.beginBlock();
829 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
830 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
831 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
832 |           return;
833 |         } else {
834 |           // Continue with the only child.
835 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
836 |         }
837 |       }
838 | 
839 |       if (count > 1) {
840 |         context = Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
841 |       }
842 |     }
843 | 
844 |     if (nodeToProcess.isEmpty()) {
845 |       cc.endStatement(true);
846 |     } else {
847 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
848 | 
849 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
850 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
851 |       if (nodeToProcess.isVar()) {
852 |         cc.endStatement();
853 |       }
854 |     }
855 |   }

===== M0118 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling

235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {

238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1240-1363
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1240 |   Node parseInputs() {
1241 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1242 | 
1243 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1244 |     // individual file parse trees.
1245 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1246 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1247 |     }
1248 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1249 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1250 |     }
1251 | 
1252 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1253 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1254 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1255 | 
1256 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1257 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1258 | 
1259 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1260 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1261 | 
1262 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1263 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1264 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1265 |     }
1266 | 
1267 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1268 | 
1269 |     try {
1270 |       // Parse externs sources.
1271 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1272 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1273 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1274 |           return null;
1275 |         }
1276 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1277 |       }
1278 | 
1279 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1280 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1281 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1282 |       }
1283 | 
1284 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1285 | 
1286 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1287 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1288 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1289 |           options.closurePass) {
1290 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1291 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1292 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1293 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1294 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1295 |           }
1296 |         }
1297 | 
1298 |         try {
1299 |           inputs =
1300 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1301 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1302 |           staleInputs = true;
1303 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1304 |           report(JSError.make(
1305 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1306 | 
1307 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1308 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1309 |             return null;
1310 |           }
1311 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1312 |           report(JSError.make(
1313 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1314 | 
1315 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1316 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1317 |             return null;
1318 |           }
1319 |         }
1320 |       }
1321 | 
1322 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1323 | 
1324 |       if (staleInputs) {
1325 |         repartitionInputs();
1326 |       }
1327 | 
1328 |       // Build the AST.
1329 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1330 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1331 |         if (n == null) {
1332 |           continue;
1333 |         }
1334 | 
1335 |         if (devMode) {
1336 |           runSanityCheck();
1337 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1338 |             return null;
1339 |           }
1340 |         }
1341 | 
1342 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1343 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1344 | 
1345 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1346 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1347 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1348 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1349 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1350 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1351 |         }
1352 | 
1353 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1354 |       }
1355 | 
1356 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1357 |         return null;
1358 |       }
1359 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1360 |     } finally {
1361 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1362 |     }
1363 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0160 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1105-1125
Documentation:

Method code:
1105 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1106 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1107 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1108 |       return s;
1109 |     }
1110 | 
1111 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1112 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1113 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1114 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1115 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1116 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1117 |       // crazy.
1118 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1119 |         sb.append(c);
1120 |       } else {
1121 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1122 |       }
1123 |     }
1124 |     return sb.toString();
1125 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 790-934
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
790 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
791 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
792 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
793 |     // that we know to be safe
794 |     switch (n.getType()) {
795 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
796 |       case Token.AND:
797 |       case Token.BLOCK:
798 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
799 |       case Token.HOOK:
800 |       case Token.IF:
801 |       case Token.IN:
802 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
803 |       case Token.NUMBER:
804 |       case Token.OR:
805 |       case Token.THIS:
806 |       case Token.TRUE:
807 |       case Token.FALSE:
808 |       case Token.NULL:
809 |       case Token.STRING:
810 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
811 |       case Token.SWITCH:
812 |       case Token.TRY:
813 |       case Token.EMPTY:
814 |         break;
815 | 
816 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
817 |       case Token.THROW:
818 |         return true;
819 | 
820 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
821 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
822 |           return true;
823 |         }
824 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
825 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
826 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
827 |             return true;
828 |           }
829 |         }
830 |         return false;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
833 |       case Token.REGEXP:
834 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
840 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
841 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
842 |           return true;
843 |         }
844 |         break;
845 | 
846 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
847 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
848 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
849 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
850 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
851 | 
852 |       case Token.NEW:
853 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
854 |           return true;
855 |         }
856 | 
857 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
858 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
859 |           // side-effects
860 |           break;
861 |         }
862 |         return true;
863 | 
864 |       case Token.CALL:
865 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
866 |         // side effect property set.
867 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
868 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
869 |           // side-effects
870 |           break;
871 |         }
872 |         return true;
873 | 
874 |       default:
875 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
876 |           break;
877 |         }
878 | 
879 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
880 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
881 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
882 |             return true;
883 |           }
884 | 
885 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
886 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
887 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
888 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
889 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
890 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
891 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
892 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
893 |             return true;
894 |           }
895 | 
896 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
897 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
898 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
899 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
900 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
901 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
902 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
903 |               return false;
904 |             }
905 | 
906 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
907 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
908 |             // other objects.
909 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
910 |             // side-effect.
911 |             while (isGet(current)) {
912 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
913 |             }
914 | 
915 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
916 |           } else {
917 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
918 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
919 |             // preserve existing behavior.
920 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
921 |           }
922 |         }
923 | 
924 |         return true;
925 |     }
926 | 
927 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
928 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
929 |         return true;
930 |       }
931 |     }
932 | 
933 |     return false;
934 |   }

88 |   private static Map<String, Annotation> buildAnnotationNames(
89 |       Set<String> annotationWhitelist) {

===== M0182 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 967-994
Documentation:

Method code:
967 |   private String jsString(String s, boolean useSlashV) {
968 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
969 | 
970 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
971 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
972 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
973 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
974 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
975 |       }
976 |     }
977 | 
978 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
979 |     char quote;
980 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
981 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
982 |       quote = '\'';
983 |       doublequote = "\"";
984 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
985 |     } else {
986 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
987 |       quote = '\"';
988 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
989 |       singlequote = "\'";
990 |     }
991 | 
992 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
993 |         outputCharsetEncoder, useSlashV);
994 |   }

===== M0187 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0193 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Token.name(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Token.java
Original range: 180-284
Documentation:

Method code:
180 |   public static String name(int token) {
181 |         switch (token) {
182 |           case ERROR:           return "ERROR";
183 |           case RETURN:          return "RETURN";
184 |           case BITOR:           return "BITOR";
185 |           case BITXOR:          return "BITXOR";
186 |           case BITAND:          return "BITAND";
187 |           case EQ:              return "EQ";
188 |           case NE:              return "NE";
189 |           case LT:              return "LT";
190 |           case LE:              return "LE";
191 |           case GT:              return "GT";
192 |           case GE:              return "GE";

===== M0202 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateFunctions(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 223-245
Documentation:
/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
229 |       for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
230 |            arg != null;
231 |            arg = arg.getNext()) {
232 |         String argName = arg.getString();
233 |         JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
234 |             null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
235 | 
236 |         if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
237 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
238 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
239 |         }
240 |         if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
241 |             typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
242 |           arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
243 |         }
244 |       }
245 |     }

===== M0216 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

===== M0221 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1708-1722
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1708 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1709 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1710 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1711 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1712 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1713 |       if (result.last != null) {
1714 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1715 |       }
1716 |       if (result.first == null) {
1717 |         result.first = n2clone;
1718 |       }
1719 |       result.last = n2clone;
1720 |     }
1721 |     return result;
1722 |   }

===== M0231 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1168-1241
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1168 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1169 |     switch (type) {
1170 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1183 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1184 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1185 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1186 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1187 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1188 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1189 |       case Token.EQ:
1190 |       case Token.NE:
1191 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1192 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1193 |       case Token.LT:
1194 |       case Token.GT:
1195 |       case Token.LE:
1196 |       case Token.GE:
1197 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1198 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1199 |       case Token.LSH:
1200 |       case Token.RSH:
1201 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1202 |       case Token.SUB:
1203 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1204 |       case Token.MUL:
1205 |       case Token.MOD:
1206 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1207 |       case Token.INC:
1208 |       case Token.DEC:
1209 |       case Token.NEW:
1210 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1211 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1212 |       case Token.VOID:
1213 |       case Token.NOT:
1214 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1215 |       case Token.POS:
1216 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1217 | 
1218 |       case Token.CALL:
1219 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1220 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1221 |       // Data values
1222 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1223 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1224 |       case Token.FALSE:
1225 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1226 |       case Token.NAME:
1227 |       case Token.NULL:
1228 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1229 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1230 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1231 |       case Token.STRING:
1232 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
1233 |       case Token.THIS:
1234 |       case Token.TRUE:
1235 |         return 15;
1236 | 
1237 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1238 |                                Token.name(type) +
1239 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1240 |     }
1241 |   }

===== M0242 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 345-371
Documentation:

Method code:
345 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
346 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
347 |     // two cases:
348 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
349 |     // and
350 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
351 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
352 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
353 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
354 |     //
355 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
356 |     //
357 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
358 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
359 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
360 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
361 |     //
362 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
363 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
364 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
365 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
366 | 
367 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
368 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
369 |     }
370 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
371 |   }

87 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1241 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1242 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1243 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1244 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {

1246 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1247 |       } else {
1248 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1249 |       }
1250 |     }
1251 |     return builder.build();
1252 |   }

===== M0265 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock(), body);
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0266 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processStringLiteral(StringLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 910-937
Documentation:

Method code:
910 |     @Override
911 |     Node processStringLiteral(StringLiteral literalNode) {
912 |       String value = literalNode.getValue();
913 |       Node n = newStringNode(value);
914 |       if (value.indexOf('\u000B') != -1) {
915 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): In JavaScript, there are 3 ways to
916 |         // represent a vertical tab: \v, \x0B, \u000B.
917 |         // The \v notation was added later, and is not understood
918 |         // on IE. So we need to preserve it as-is. This is really
919 |         // obnoxious, because we do not have a good way to represent
920 |         // how the original string was encoded without making the
921 |         // representation of strings much more complicated.
922 |         //
923 |         // To handle this, we look at the original source test, and
924 |         // mark the string as \v-encoded or not. If a string is
925 |         // \v encoded, then all the vertical tabs in that string
926 |         // will be encoded with a \v.
927 |         int start = literalNode.getAbsolutePosition();
928 |         int end = start + literalNode.getLength();
929 |         if (start < sourceString.length() &&
930 |             (sourceString.substring(
931 |                  start, Math.min(sourceString.length(), end))
932 |              .indexOf("\\v") != -1)) {
933 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.SLASH_V, true);
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 |       return n;
937 |     }

===== M0285 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isExpressionResultUsed(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3110-3149
Documentation:
/**
   * @returns false iff the result of the expression is not consumed.
   */
Method code:
3110 |   static boolean isExpressionResultUsed(Node expr) {
3111 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider sharing some code with trySimpleUnusedResult.
3112 |     Node parent = expr.getParent();
3113 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
3114 |       case Token.BLOCK:
3115 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
3116 |         return false;
3117 |       case Token.HOOK:
3118 |       case Token.AND:
3119 |       case Token.OR:
3120 |         return (expr == parent.getFirstChild())
3121 |             ? true : isExpressionResultUsed(parent);
3122 |       case Token.COMMA:
3123 |         Node gramps = parent.getParent();
3124 |         if (gramps.isCall() &&
3125 |             parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
3126 |           // Semantically, a direct call to eval is different from an indirect
3127 |           // call to an eval. See ECMA-262 S15.1.2.1. So it's OK for the first
3128 |           // expression to a comma to be a no-op if it's used to indirect
3129 |           // an eval. This we pretend that this is "used".
3130 |           if (expr == parent.getFirstChild() &&
3131 |               parent.getChildCount() == 2 &&
3132 |               expr.getNext().isName() &&
3133 |               "eval".equals(expr.getNext().getString())) {
3134 |             return true;
3135 |           }
3136 |         }
3137 | 
3138 |         return (expr == parent.getFirstChild())
3139 |             ? false : isExpressionResultUsed(parent);
3140 |       case Token.FOR:
3141 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
3142 |           // Only an expression whose result is in the condition part of the
3143 |           // expression is used.
3144 |           return (parent.getChildAtIndex(1) == expr);
3145 |         }
3146 |         break;
3147 |     }
3148 |     return true;
3149 |   }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

463 |     @Override
464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
465 |       if (lineBreak) {
466 |         if (sawFunction) {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
473 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
474 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
475 |       int len = code.length();
476 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
477 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
478 |         if (ch == ';') {
479 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |       maybeCutLine();
483 |     }

===== M0320 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 128-179
Documentation:

Method code:
128 |     @Override
129 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
130 |       // null is the smallest value
131 |       if (p2 == null) {
132 |         if (p1 == null) {
133 |           return 0;
134 |         } else {
135 |           return P1_GT_P2;
136 |         }
137 |       }
138 | 
139 |       // check level
140 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
141 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
142 |       }
143 | 
144 |       // sourceName comparison
145 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
146 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
147 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
148 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
149 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
150 |           return sourceCompare;
151 |         }
152 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
153 |         return P1_LT_P2;
154 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
155 |         return P1_GT_P2;
156 |       }
157 |       // lineno comparison
158 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
159 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
160 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
161 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
162 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
163 |         return P1_LT_P2;
164 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
165 |         return P1_GT_P2;
166 |       }
167 |       // charno comparison
168 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
169 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
170 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
171 |         return charno1 - charno2;
172 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
173 |         return P1_LT_P2;
174 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
175 |         return P1_GT_P2;
176 |       }
177 |       // description
178 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
179 |     }

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1016-1103
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape JavaScript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1016 |   private static String strEscape(
1017 |       String s, char quote,
1018 |       String doublequoteEscape,
1019 |       String singlequoteEscape,
1020 |       String backslashEscape,
1021 |       CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder,
1022 |       boolean useSlashV) {
1023 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1024 |     sb.append(quote);
1025 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1026 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1027 |       switch (c) {
1028 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1029 |         case '\u000B':
1030 |           if (useSlashV) {
1031 |             sb.append("\\v");
1032 |           } else {
1033 |             sb.append("\\x0B");
1034 |           }
1035 |           break;
1036 |         // From the SingleEscapeCharacter grammar production.
1037 |         case '\b': sb.append("\\b"); break;
1038 |         case '\f': sb.append("\\f"); break;
1039 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1040 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1041 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1042 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1043 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1044 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1045 | 
1046 |         // From LineTerminators (ES5 Section 7.3, Table 3)
1047 |         case '\u2028': sb.append("\\u2028"); break;
1048 |         case '\u2029': sb.append("\\u2029"); break;
1049 | 
1050 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1051 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1052 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1053 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1054 |             sb.append("\\>");
1055 |           } else {
1056 |             sb.append(c);
1057 |           }
1058 |           break;
1059 |         case '<':
1060 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1061 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1062 | 
1063 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1064 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1065 | 
1066 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1067 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1068 |             sb.append("<\\");
1069 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1070 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1071 |             sb.append("<\\");
1072 |           } else {
1073 |             sb.append(c);
1074 |           }
1075 |           break;
1076 |         default:
1077 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1078 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1079 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1080 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1081 |               sb.append(c);
1082 |             } else {
1083 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1084 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1085 |             }
1086 |           } else {
1087 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight Latin characters
1088 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1089 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1090 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1091 |               sb.append(c);
1092 |             } else {
1093 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some JS parsers,
1094 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1095 |               // so we play it safe and Unicode escape them.
1096 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1097 |             }
1098 |           }
1099 |       }
1100 |     }
1101 |     sb.append(quote);
1102 |     return sb.toString();
1103 |   }

===== M0366 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 246-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
246 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
247 |     this.options = options;
248 |     if (errorManager == null) {
249 |       if (outStream == null) {
250 |         setErrorManager(
251 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
252 |       } else {
253 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
254 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
255 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
256 |         setErrorManager(printer);
257 |       }
258 |     }
259 | 
260 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
261 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
262 |       options.checkTypes = true;
263 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
264 |       options.checkTypes = false;
265 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
266 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
267 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
268 |       // parser type warnings.
269 |       options.setWarningLevel(
270 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
271 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
272 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
273 |     }
274 | 
275 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
276 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
277 |       options.setWarningLevel(
278 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
279 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
283 |       options.setWarningLevel(
284 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
285 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
289 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
290 |     guards.add(
291 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
292 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
293 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
294 | 
295 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
296 | 
297 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
298 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
299 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
300 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
301 |     // error off.
302 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
303 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
304 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
305 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
306 |     }
307 | 
308 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
309 |   }

/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
713 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
714 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
715 |         "The new child node has siblings.");

717 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.getNonEmptyChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1131-1142
Documentation:
/**
   * @param maxCount The maximum number of children to look for.
   * @return The number of children of this node that are non empty up to
   * maxCount.
   */
Method code:
1131 |   private static int getNonEmptyChildCount(Node n, int maxCount) {
1132 |     int i = 0;
1133 |     Node c = n.getFirstChild();
1134 |     for (; c != null && i < maxCount; c = c.getNext()) {
1135 |       if (c.isBlock()) {
1136 |         i += getNonEmptyChildCount(c, maxCount-i);
1137 |       } else if (!c.isEmpty()) {
1138 |         i++;
1139 |       }
1140 |     }
1141 |     return i;
1142 |   }

===== M0452 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1444-1463
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

773 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {

154 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {

175 |         return;
176 |

178 |       case Token.COMMA:

200 |       case Token.MOD:

203 |         return;

219 |         return;

226 |       case Token.STRING:

===== M0493 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 861-881
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the Node is a DO or FUNCTION (with or without
   * labels).
   */
Method code:
861 |   private boolean isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node n) {
862 |     if (n.isLabel()) {
863 |       Node labeledStatement = n.getLastChild();
864 |       if (!labeledStatement.isBlock()) {
865 |         return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(labeledStatement);
866 |       } else {
867 |         // For labels with block children, we need to ensure that a
868 |         // labeled FUNCTION or DO isn't generated when extraneous BLOCKs
869 |         // are skipped.
870 |         if (getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2) == 1) {
871 |           return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(getFirstNonEmptyChild(n));
872 |         } else {
873 |           // Either a empty statement or an block with more than one child,
874 |           // way it isn't a FUNCTION or DO.
875 |           return false;
876 |         }
877 |       }
878 |     } else {
879 |       return (n.isFunction() || n.isDo());
880 |     }
881 |   }
