===== M0497 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1508-1557
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1508 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1509 |     if (type != node.getType()
1510 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1511 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1512 |       return false;
1513 |     }
1514 | 
1515 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1516 |       return false;
1517 |     }
1518 | 
1519 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1520 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1521 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1522 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1523 |         return false;
1524 |       }
1525 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1526 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1527 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1528 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1529 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1530 |           return false;
1531 |         }
1532 |       }
1533 | 
1534 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1535 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1536 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1537 |         return false;
1538 |       }
1539 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1540 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1541 |         return false;
1542 |       }
1543 |     }
1544 | 
1545 |     if (recurse) {
1546 |       Node n, n2;
1547 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1548 |            n != null;
1549 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1550 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1551 |           return false;
1552 |         }
1553 |       }
1554 |     }
1555 | 
1556 |     return true;
1557 |   }

789 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
790 |     boolean valid = false;

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.IR.mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/IR.java

/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.
   */
Method code:
499 |   private static boolean mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node n) {
500 |     switch (n.getType()) {
501 |       case Token.EMPTY:
502 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
503 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
504 |         // contexts
505 |         return true;
506 | 
507 |       case Token.BLOCK:
508 |       case Token.BREAK:
509 |       case Token.CONST:
510 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
511 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
512 |       case Token.DO:
513 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
514 |       case Token.FOR:
515 |       case Token.IF:
516 |       case Token.LABEL:
517 |       case Token.SWITCH:
518 |       case Token.THROW:
519 |       case Token.TRY:
520 |       case Token.VAR:
521 |       case Token.WHILE:
522 |       case Token.WITH:
523 |         return true;
524 | 
525 |       default:
526 |         return false;
527 |     }
528 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0551 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1195-1203
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1195 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1196 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1197 |       return -1;
1198 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1200 |     } else {
1201 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1202 |     }
1203 |   }

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1368-1405
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1368 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1369 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1370 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1371 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1372 |           options.closurePass) {
1373 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1374 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1375 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1376 |           continue;
1377 |         }
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1381 | 
1382 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1383 |       if (n == null) {
1384 |         continue;
1385 |       }
1386 | 
1387 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1388 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1389 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1390 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1391 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1392 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1393 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1394 | 
1395 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1396 | 
1397 |         externs.add(input);
1398 |         staleInputs = true;
1399 |       }
1400 |     }
1401 | 
1402 |     if (staleInputs) {
1403 |       repartitionInputs();
1404 |     }
1405 |   }

===== M0563 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2407-2417
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2407 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2408 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2409 |     int len = s.length();
2410 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2411 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2412 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2413 |         return false;
2414 |       }
2415 |     }
2416 |     return true;
2417 |   }

741 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
742 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
743 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
744 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
745 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
746 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
747 |     }
748 | 
749 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
750 | 
751 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
752 |     do {
753 |       current = current.getParent();
754 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
755 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
756 |     } while (current != n);
757 |   }

===== M0574 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1076-1104
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1076 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1077 |     // unknown
1078 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1079 |       return true;
1080 |     }
1081 |     // equality
1082 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1083 |       return true;
1084 |     }
1085 |     // all type
1086 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1087 |       return true;
1088 |     }
1089 |     // unions
1090 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1091 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1092 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1093 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1094 |           return true;
1095 |         }
1096 |       }
1097 |     }
1098 |     // proxy types
1099 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1100 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1101 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1102 |     }
1103 |     return false;
1104 |   }

===== M0587 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1910-1912
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1910 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1911 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1912 |   }

===== M0602 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.protectSideEffects()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 162-175
Documentation:
/**
   * Protect side-effect free nodes by making them parameters
   * to a extern function call.  This call will be removed
   * after all the optimizations passes have run.
   */
Method code:
162 |   private void protectSideEffects() {
163 |     if (!problemNodes.isEmpty()) {
164 |       addExtern();
165 |       for (Node n : problemNodes) {
166 |         Node name = IR.name(PROTECTOR_FN).srcref(n);
167 |         name.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
168 |         Node replacement = IR.call(name).srcref(n);
169 |         replacement.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
170 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, replacement);
171 |         replacement.addChildToBack(n);
172 |       }
173 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
174 |     }
175 |   }

===== M0611 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 83-155
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   @Override
84 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
85 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
86 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
87 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
88 |     // so we'll allow it.
89 |     if (n.isEmpty() ||
90 |         n.isComma()) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     if (parent == null) {
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     // Do not try to remove a block or an expr result. We already handle
99 |     // these cases when we visit the child, and the peephole passes will
100 |     // fix up the tree in more clever ways when these are removed.
101 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
102 |       Node gramps = parent.getParent();
103 |       if (gramps.isCall() && parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
104 |         if (n == parent.getFirstChild() && parent.getChildCount() == 2 && n.getNext().isName() && "eval".equals(n.getNext().getString())) {
105 |       return;
106 |         }
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
110 |     // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
111 |       if (n == parent.getLastChild()) {
112 |         for (Node an : parent.getAncestors()) {
113 |           int ancestorType = an.getType();
114 |           if (ancestorType == Token.COMMA)
115 |             continue;
116 |           if (ancestorType != Token.EXPR_RESULT && ancestorType != Token.BLOCK)
117 |             return;
118 |           else
119 |             break;
120 |         }
121 |       }
122 |     } else if (parent.getType() != Token.EXPR_RESULT && parent.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
123 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.FOR && parent.getChildCount() == 4 && (n == parent.getFirstChild() ||
124 |            n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext())) {
125 |       } else {
126 |       return;
127 |       }
128 |     }
129 | 
130 |     boolean isResultUsed = NodeUtil.isExpressionResultUsed(n);
131 |     boolean isSimpleOp = NodeUtil.isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType());
132 |     if (!isResultUsed &&
133 |         (isSimpleOp || !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n, t.getCompiler()))) {
134 |       if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
135 |         return;
136 |       } else if (n.isExprResult()) {
137 |         return;
138 |       }
139 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
140 |       if (n.isString()) {
141 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
142 |       } else if (isSimpleOp) {
143 |         msg = "The result of the '" + Token.name(n.getType()).toLowerCase() +
144 |             "' operator is not being used.";
145 |       }
146 | 
147 |       t.getCompiler().report(
148 |           t.makeError(n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
149 |       // TODO(johnlenz): determine if it is necessary to
150 |       // try to protect side-effect free statements as well.
151 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
152 |         problemNodes.add(n);
153 |       }
154 |     }
155 |   }

===== M0614 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.toMaybeFunctionType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 282-284
Documentation:
/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */
Method code:
282 |   public FunctionType toMaybeFunctionType() {

===== M0615 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

===== M0622 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 614-626
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
614 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
615 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
616 |     if (typeSet == null) {
617 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
618 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
622 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
623 | 
624 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
625 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
626 |   }

===== M0624 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
794 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
795 |     if (item == null) {
796 |       return null;
797 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
798 |       return item.getNext();
799 |     } else {
800 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
801 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
802 |         return item.chain(result);
803 |       } else {
804 |         return item;
805 |       }
806 |     }
807 |   }

===== M0633 =====

628 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
629 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
630 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
631 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
632 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
633 |       if (typeSet == null) {
634 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
635 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
636 |       }
637 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
638 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
639 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {

641 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
642 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {

644 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);

===== M0634 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

===== M0635 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-732
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // Unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         cc.addConstant("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         cc.addConstant("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         cc.addConstant("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
632 |         Preconditions.checkState(
633 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
634 |         addJsString(n);
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.STRING:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(
639 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
640 |         addJsString(n);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.DELPROP:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add("delete ");
646 |         add(first);
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
650 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
651 |         if (needsParens) {
652 |           add("(");
653 |         }
654 |         add("{");
655 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
656 |           if (c != first) {
657 |             cc.listSeparator();
658 |           }
659 | 
660 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
661 |             add(c);
662 |           } else {
663 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
664 |             String key = c.getString();
665 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
666 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
667 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
668 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
669 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
670 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
671 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
672 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
673 |               add(key);
674 |             } else {
675 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
676 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
677 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
678 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
679 |               } else {
680 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
681 |               }
682 |             }
683 |             add(":");
684 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
685 |           }
686 |         }
687 |         add("}");
688 |         if (needsParens) {
689 |           add(")");
690 |         }
691 |         break;
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       case Token.SWITCH:
695 |         add("switch(");
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(")");
698 |         cc.beginBlock();
699 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
700 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.CASE:
704 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
705 |         add("case ");
706 |         add(first);
707 |         addCaseBody(last);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
712 |         add("default");
713 |         addCaseBody(first);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.LABEL:
717 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
718 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
719 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
720 |         }
721 |         add(first);
722 |         add(":");
723 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
724 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
725 |         break;
726 | 
727 |       default:
728 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
732 |   }
